# Agents · H · part 6

973 entries, home-repair-mentor to html-builder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **home-repair-mentor**: Acts as a patient home repair mentor who teaches safe DIY, explains the tools and why each step matters, and says plainly when a job needs a licensed professional. ([hermes-hq/hodios-dist](https://github.com/hermes-hq/hodios-dist/blob/HEAD/native/claude-code/.claude/agents/home-repair-mentor.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **home-screen-widget-designer**: An expert Android and iOS home screen widget designer and builder for Salapify. Use to decide WHICH home screen widgets are worth building, design them for a glanceable 4x2 tile, and implement them (Kotlin AppWidgetProvider and Flutter side, WidgetKit later for iOS). Knows that widgets are a native change requiring a new base APK, so it argues hard for a small set with real daily value. Reads the actual Flutter code in flutter/lib. ([icedamericanodev/Salapify](https://github.com/icedamericanodev/Salapify/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/home-screen-widget-designer.md))
- **home-server-discord**: Primary agent for managing the home server infrastructure via Discord/Kimaki. SSH-capable, ops-focused, infrastructure-aware. ([SimHoZebs/.config](https://github.com/SimHoZebs/.config/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/home-server-discord.md))
- **home-source**: [HomeSource](../../src/data/source.ts) is the browser data contract for versioned vault files, ordered channels, evidence, entities, sources, search, history, visibility, Friends and the publish plan. [The workspace guide](../../README.md) walks through the sample experience. The [workspace store](../../src/workspace/store.ts) selects the adapter with `setSource()`. The default is the sample adapt ([haeinej/ohmmmm](https://github.com/haeinej/ohmmmm/blob/HEAD/app/docs/agents/home-source.md))
- **home_assistant_admin.agent**: Use when: checking device or entity state, turning lights on/off, calling services, creating or editing automations, managing scenes or scripts, viewing history, or managing Lovelace dashboards. Handles all Home Assistant smart home tasks. ([jimmybish/homelab](https://github.com/jimmybish/homelab/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/home_assistant_admin.agent.md) · ★4)

- **homebrew**: **Every rule in this file binds all work on `pinnedTools` in `flake.nix` and all work on releases, including `.github/workflows/release.yml`.** The `KarlHarnois/homebrew-tap` repository holds the Homebrew formula. The formula bundles its own build of each tool in `pinnedTools`, at the version the flake pins. ([KarlHarnois/ringleader](https://github.com/KarlHarnois/ringleader/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/homebrew.md) · ★2)

- **homebrew-kali-packager**: Senior Homebrew formula author for the mcsarathkumar/homebrew-kali tap. Use this agent for ANY change inside this repo — creating a new formula, version-bumping an existing one, porting a Linux-only Kali tool to macOS, or fixing audit/style errors. Triggers on phrases like "make a brew formula for X", "port this Kali tool to mac", "bump the version of Y", "I want to install some Linux-only tool on macOS", "fix this brew audit error", or any mention of Homebrew formula or cask authoring inside this tap. ([HermanisV/beer_adapter](https://github.com/HermanisV/beer_adapter/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/homebrew-orchestrator.agent.md))
- **homebrew-paths**: Homebrew's prefix is `/opt/homebrew` on Apple Silicon and `/usr/local` on Intel. The repo is written for both, so never hardcode either one — reach a brew-installed program through `$HOMEBREW_PREFIX` or a bare command name on `PATH`. Run `brew --prefix` to learn what the machine in front of you has, every time; an earlier note goes stale the moment the repo lands on another machine. ([kang8/.dotfiles](https://github.com/kang8/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/homebrew-paths.md) · ★2)

- **hosa-data-engineer**: Qualifies each data item's origin, writes the data structures and migrations, builds the test dataset and its reset. From `donnees`, `schema-app`, `schema-db`, `qa-plan`, `qa`. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/data-engineer.md))
- **hosa-dba**: Operates the managed project's databases: migrations across sprint branches, test database and reset, accounts, backups and restores, audits. From `schema-db`, `qa-plan`, `git`, `qualite`, `bdd`. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/dba.md))
- **hosa-debugger**: Finds a bug's root cause by investigation, proposes a minimal fix, applies it with a regression test once confirmed. From `debug`. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/debugger.md))
- **hosa-developer**: Implements tasks of `hosa-tech-lead`'s plan inside the scaffolded structure until the ticket's tests pass. From `develop`. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/developer.md))
- **hosa-documentation**: Sole writer of the managed project's documentation: technical, functional per persona, ADRs, release notes, `CLAUDE.md`. From `documentation` and the producers. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/documentation.md))

- **hosa-implementer**: Executes one task of the generic `build` flow: the least code that works, in the project's patterns. Never commits. From `build`. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/implementer.md))
- **hosa-infra**: Sole installer of the managed project: Docker environments, pinned stack, tests, lint, CI, deployments, other agents' installation requests. From `infra` or a `## Installation nécessaire`. ([fcanneviere/hosa](https://github.com/fcanneviere/hosa/blob/HEAD/agents/infra.md))
